# Commands · B · part 6

1,502 entries, bridge to bugfix. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **bridge**: Read and answer the VS Code ⇄ Cloud mailbox in BRIDGE.md, or post a new message to the other side. ([tsiradeghyan-creator/my-first-project](https://github.com/tsiradeghyan-creator/my-first-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bridge.md))
- **bridge**: Bridge sub-operations: skills|hooks|mcp|mirrors|all (default all). Symlinks the plugin's skills, mirrors the project's Claude hooks into .codex/ for the five events both tools share, and mirrors .mcp.json servers into config.toml — never copying secret values. mirrors regenerates the codex/ tree via scripts/mirror-sync.py (E7.2). ([xinquan568/vibe-suite](https://github.com/xinquan568/vibe-suite/blob/HEAD/commands/bridge.md))
- **bridge**: Use when a task requires changes in a DIFFERENT git repository than the one you're working in — rather than cd-ing away, open one persistent claude/codex worker inside that other repo (repo B) and co-develop with it over open-ended rounds, relaying questions both ways with the user, finishing as a PR there. ([yangbinchen/agglomeration-platform](https://github.com/yangbinchen/agglomeration-platform/blob/HEAD/commands/bridge.md) · 2 copies)

Mutating admin commands stay on ``scripts/ops/bridge-admin.sh``. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/bridge-deposits.md))

- **buddy:consolidate**: Consolidate accumulated memories — merge near-duplicates, archive stale entries, summarize tag-clusters, surface contradictions for resolution. Runs as a four-phase pipeline (rules shortlist → specialist judgment → user dry-run gate → apply). Pass a target (specialist alias, `common`, `all`) or one of the sub-commands `apply`/`revise &lt;text&gt;`/`cancel`. With no argument, consolidates memories of currently active specialists. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/consolidate.md) · ★7)
- **buddy:create**: Create a new buddy specialist (global or project scope; builtin is frozen). Brainstorms with the user, drafts from the canonical skill template, previews what /buddy:summon will load (template + memory protocol + gates), then writes. Discovery is path-scan — no registration step needed. The argument is a freeform hint (e.g. "buddy for X", "buddy for X project") or empty. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/create.md) · ★7)
- **buddy:introspect**: Run mid-session introspection on one or all currently summoned specialists. Unlike `/buddy:dismiss`, the specialist stays active afterward. Pass a specialist name to introspect just that one (e.g. `/buddy:introspect hamsa`); pass nothing to introspect all active. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/introspect.md) · ★7)
- **buddy:remember**: Ask the currently active specialist(s) to save a memory about the given lesson. Pass the lesson as the argument — e.g. `/buddy:remember in this repo, integration tests must hit a real database`. The specialist decides global vs project scope and the slug. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/remember.md) · ★7)
- **buddy:summon**: Summon a specialist bodhisattva to help with a specific craft. Describe who you need in plain language — e.g. "debug", "testing", "ML training", "architecture", "security", "refactor", "performance", "docs", "data leakage classic", "data leakage llm", "planning", "prompt". Some specialists have lenses; pass them as `&lt;specialist&gt;:&lt;lens&gt;` (e.g. `data-leakage:llm`). An ambiguous argument prints the specialist table and exits without loading anything. ([mareurs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/mareurs/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/buddy/commands/summon.md) · ★7)
